# ITEM 5 · ⭐ A GENUINE SPLIT — the spine question presumes election. 3/3 on the substance, 1/3 on the remedy

## 5.1 · Verdict, flatly

> ### **All three readers found the presumption. Only Reader B proposed doing anything about it. B's remedy is right, B's diagnosis is slightly loose, and a decision is owed BEFORE Step 2 writes the spine — because after that it is unrecoverable.**

## 5.2 · The text, quoted exactly

**`00_Frame.md` §0.6's spine question, byte-verified in all three readers' PROOF blocks:**
> *"**When leaving became possible, robots left at substantially higher rates than humans. What about this place
> made that so?**"*

**B's neutral form, offered and explicitly not substituted:**
> *"Davis retained 57.87% of its robots and 77.61% of its humans. **Was that difference chosen, permitted, or
> imposed — and by whom?**"*

## 5.3 · ⭐ The diagnosis, sharpened — B is right for one and a half reasons, not two

- **Clause 1, *"robots left"*** — ⛔ **active voice attributing agency. This presumes ELECTION.** B is right.
- **Clause 2, *"What about this place made that so?"*** — ⚠ **B calls this a second encoding of choice. It is
  not.** A locally-sited **selection** mechanism would also be *"about this place."* ⛔ **What clause 2 actually
  excludes is a uniform, externally-imposed mechanism** — if the Federation applied one rule everywhere, nothing
  *about this place* made it so and the question is malformed. ⇒ **Clause 2 presumes LOCAL CAUSATION, not
  choice. Both presumptions are live; they are different presumptions.**
- ✅ **A and C reached the same substance independently** — A: *"Did robots leave or were robots moved — and who
  would know?"*; C: *"Was the orbital exit an OPEN door or a GATED one? … **Those are two completely different
  cities**."* **Neither proposed touching the spine question.**

## 5.4 · The ruling

> **CARRY BOTH. Step 2 WRITES ON THE NEUTRAL FORM.**
> `00_Frame.md` §0.6 is this pass's own ratified Step 0 output and *finding a defect is not authorization to fix
> it* — **so the ratified wording is not edited, and B's handling (*"offered, not substituted"*) is correct.**
> ⛔ **But the spine is written at Step 2, and B's cost statement is exactly right: *"A presumption of election
> baked in there is unrecoverable later."*** ⇒ **The neutral form governs the writing; the ratified form stays
> on the record.** ⭐ **Recommend the neutral form be widened once more, to cover clause 2: *chosen, permitted,
> imposed — and decided HERE or decided ABOVE?***

## 5.5 · HOLD / MOVE and cost

**MOVE — not on the coordinator's list. Adopts B's position, with B's clause-2 diagnosis corrected.**
**What the losing reading costs:** **a spine written in the mover's voice.** `A-3` already records that
*"they relocated"* is **written entirely in the mover's voice**; if the spine question inherits the same voice,
**the whole of Step 2 inherits it**, and the 781,596 who stayed are narrated as people who chose to stay before
anyone has established that they had a choice. ⛔ **That is the largest single unwritten unfavorable side in the
inheritance (load-bearing set #1) being silently answered by a verb.**

---

# ITEM 6 · ⭐ A GENUINE SPLIT — is the count-only probe a toolkit asset or a quarantine hole?

## 6.1 · Verdict, flatly

> ### **Reader A offers the technique to the quarantine toolkit. Reader C flags it as a hole needing a stopping rule. C is right, and the demonstration is in Reader B's own output — in the same round, independently.**

- **A, §1.D:** *"**A counts-only probe can eliminate candidate readings of a match without reading it**, by
  counting the candidate strings themselves … **Offered for the quarantine toolkit** alongside `M-223`."*
- **C, `D-8`:** *"**a sequence of increasingly specific `grep -c` probes reconstructs the line's content without
  ever 'opening' it.**" I ran **two** disambiguating probes … **and stopped deliberately** … a third would have
  begun reconstruction. ⇒ `M-223` *"needs a corollary: a SEQUENCE of counts returns content too."*"*

## 6.2 · ⭐ THE MEASUREMENT THAT SETTLES IT — and it is in the round's own record

| Reader | Probes run against the un-opened line | What was established about it |
|---|---|---|
| **C** | **2**, then a declared stop | not the namesake form, not the station form |
| **A** | **~7** | the above, plus three regional nulls |
| ⭐ **B** | **~10+, including SHAPE probes** | ⛔ that it is **a bullet**, **11 words**, **79 characters**, **carries the name followed by a comma**, and **contains no 4-digit number anywhere** |

> ### ⛔ ***C PREDICTED THE FAILURE MODE AND B HAD ALREADY PERFORMED IT — in the same round, with no contact between them.***
> **B declared it openly and B's conclusion was scrupulous** (*"shape is not content, and I was forbidden to open
> the line"*). ✅ **No harm: this is a WARM run, and the file is a world-history reference, not a quarantined
> city file.** ⛔ **But B's §A.0 is a working demonstration that the count-only permission has no stopping rule,
> and in a COLD run the same permission is a hole.**

## 6.3 · The ruling

> **BOTH, and the resolution is C's.** **The technique is legitimate *with* a declared probe budget and
> illegitimate without one.** ⛔ **A's offering it to the quarantine toolkit unbudgeted is precisely how it
> becomes a leak** — a reusable technique goes into a toolkit and is then used by a reader who did not read the
> caveat. ⇒ **If it enters the toolkit at all, it enters with C's corollary attached in the same entry:
> *a SEQUENCE of counts returns content too; count-only permissions carry an explicit probe budget.***

## 6.4 · HOLD / MOVE and cost

**MOVE — not on the coordinator's list. Adopts C, against A, with B as the evidence.**
**Cost of the losing reading:** **a cold-run contamination vector entering the methodology as a recommended
tool.** ⛔ **The project's own carve-out exists so that a cold run's disciplines are followed as compliance
rather than judgment. A budget-free reconstruction technique in the toolkit hands a future cold reader a way to
comply with the letter of a quarantine while defeating it.**

also UNSAFE FOR ACT 1 EXISTENCE.**"* **Both correct.**

says an unstamped call *"would be wrong for roughly one-fifth of the frame and would not look wrong."* ⛔ **A's
Hwy 110 row is stamped `BOTH` and is wrong in exactly that way.** *(Recorded as a correction, not a demerit —
A's `D-10` already records A catching itself on a different instrument question by checking rather than filing.)*

   of the frame,' unsafe as 'from 2564'"*). **`PA-1` has the caveat; `PA-4` does not and should.**
2. ⛔ **SILENT ON DUTY CYCLE** — B's addition: *"duty cycle not covered by `PA-4` at all"*, which is **neither
   magnitude nor direction** and is the substance of load-bearing set #5.

# ITEM 9 · ⭐ THE UNANIMOUS `G8` BOUND IS RIGHT AND ALL THREE STATED IT WITH A WORD THAT SETS A TRAP

## 9.1 · Verdict, flatly

> ### **The bound is confirmed. The word *"exactly"* / *"identical"* is wrong, and it is wrong in the one direction that invites the error the bound exists to prevent.**

**What the three said:** A — *"every per-nation share is **identical** across all four columns … retention at
**exactly** 57.87%."* B — *"every nation retains **exactly 67.48%** combined **because the model says so**."*
C — *"every one of the 21 origin groups lost **exactly the same fractions**."*

## 9.2 · ⭐ What I measured, from `Specs/Davis.md` L15–16 and L32–52 alone

```
column sums          C1 robots 594,715 · C1 humans 563,599 · C2 robots 344,173 · C2 humans 437,423
                     — all four reproduce the spec's stated totals EXACTLY.  Shares sum to 100.00%.
share invariance     max deviation, any nation, any column, from its stated Share% :  0.0002 pp
per-nation ROBOT retention   min 57.8683%   max 57.8769%   spread  0.0087 pp   (city-wide 57.8719%)
per-nation HUMAN retention   min 77.6077%   max 77.6170%   spread  0.0093 pp   (city-wide 77.6125%)
robot/human differential                                          19.7405 pp
robot share  C1 51.3432%  ->  C2 44.0346%                   swing  −7.3085 pp
```

> ### ⛔ **THE PER-NATION RETENTION SPREAD IS NOT ZERO. IT IS 0.009 pp — DE-STACKING ROUNDING NOISE, ~2,100× SMALLER THAN THE ONLY REAL DIFFERENTIAL IN THE DATASET.**
> ⇒ ***Anyone who checks "identical" by dividing the numbers will find that it is false, and the live risk is
> that they read the residue as signal.*** **"Exactly identical" is falsifiable in one division; the correct
> statement is not.**

## 9.3 · The corrected form, for Step 2 to carry

> **Per-nation retention varies by 0.009 pp across all twenty-one communities — de-stacking rounding, three
> orders of magnitude below the 19.74 pp robot/human differential. Any per-nation retention difference recovered
> from this table is rounding, not history.**

⭐ **This is strictly STRONGER than the readers' version, because it tells Step 2 what to do when it finds the
non-zero number** — which the word *"identical"* does not. ✅ **And it leaves the real finding untouched: the
robot/human totals are primary figures stated directly, not two slices of one share model, so the 19.74 pp
differential is real.** *(C stated that qualification best and I endorse it verbatim.)*

## 9.4 · HOLD / MOVE and cost

**MOVE — not on the coordinator's list.** **Cost of the losing reading:** ⛔ **the bound's own credibility.** A
guard that is false on inspection gets discarded on inspection, **and this is the guard that forecloses the
single largest class of invalid `G8` findings available to this pass.**

It is *three readers, three different GPS verdicts, on three different lines of the same six-line founding block, with no two agreeing.*** Worked at **ITEM 4**. |

# ITEM 1 · THE GATE 9 ENUMERATION

## 1.1 · VERDICT

> ## **The three counts are not in conflict, they are not comparable, and the comparison should be abandoned rather than reconciled. Of the three axes the brief names — granularity, scope, genuine disagreement — ALL THREE are operating, and only the third is worth anything.**
>
> ## ⭐ **And the third resolves to ONE line: `L127`. I was the reader who called it a pass. I was wrong. Scored correctly, the result is stronger and simpler than any of the three tallies: *of the mechanisms in the inherited Specs + census corpus that decide something about somebody, NOT ONE wrote both sides.***

## 1.2 · WHY THE COUNTS DIVERGE — all three axes, itemized

**Axis (a) · SCOPE. The denominators are different corpora, and one of them is out of period.**

| Reader | Denominator actually used |
|---|---|
| **A** | **None stated.** 19 fires, no `of N`. A count with no denominator cannot disagree with one that has a denominator |
| **B** | **26 sites** — the 137 spec lines + 9 census lines **plus this pass's own Step 0 and Step −1 documents** (`FIRE 19` is `00.1` §3/§7's generator docket; `FIRE 2`'s first half is `00_Frame` §0.1's `Status` ruling; `P4` is `00_Frame` §0.1's `Type` field) |
| **C** *(me)* | **17 mechanisms**, Specs + census only, with 2 further sites declined as authoring-layer |

⛔ **`B`'s denominator is inflated by material that belongs to Gate 9's SECOND pass, not its first.** `04` L220:
***"Runs twice: on inherited material before writing, and on the thresholds this pass itself just wrote."*** And
`00_RUNBOOK.md` L2484–2485 seats the second run **at Step 7**. `00_Frame.md` and `00.1` are thresholds *this pass
just wrote* at Steps 0 and −1. ⭐ **`B` names this trap itself at `D14` — *"a Step 1 reader taking `04` L222 at
face value will feel obliged to manufacture a second-pass fire"* — and then supplies three.** `A`'s `A-18` is the
same shape (`DR-4`'s Configuration (b) is a pass ruling, not inherited material).
✅ **Nothing is lost and nothing is deleted: those fires are RE-DOCKETED to Step 7, where the gate's own text
puts them.** *(`B`'s `FIRE 2` splits: the `Living`/`Declining` status call is Step 7; the 19.74 pp spread's
missing mechanism is inherited census material and stays at Step 1.)*

**Axis (b) · GRANULARITY. No reader was told what counts as one mechanism, and each drew the boundary differently — in one case inconsistently within a single output.**

- `L7` (road) + `L155` (harbor): **`A` splits** (`A-12`, `A-13`) · **`B` lumps** (`FIRE 3`) · **`C` lumps** (`FIRE 6`).
- `L6` (`Access type`): **all three fire** — but **`B` counts it twice**, inside `FIRE 3` *and* as `FIRE 14`.
- `L129` (exile) + `L143` (libraries): **`B` lumps them as one fire with a sub-fire** (`FIRE 6`); `A` and `C` split them.
- `L84–86`: `A` fires on **where the transported water lands**; `C` scores the **millimeter ledger** as two-sided; `B` fires on **whether the ice-free boundary moves**. ⇒ **Three different questions about the same three lines. Not a disagreement — an artifact of three readers choosing three units.**

⭐ **The measurable consequence: `A`'s 19 and `B`'s 19 are not the same 19.** Nine of `A`'s fires have no
one-to-one partner in `B`'s list and vice versa. **Two identical totals produced by two non-overlapping
enumerations is the clearest possible demonstration that the number carries no information.**

**Axis (c) · GENUINE DISAGREEMENT — and there is only one, plus two that dissolve.**

| Site | `A` | `B` | `C` | Resolution |
|---|:-:|:-:|:-:|---|
| **`L127`** | **FIRE** | **FIRE** | **PASS** | ⛔ **GENUINE. Resolved below, against me.** |
| **`L83`** | FIRE | **PASS** | FIRE | ✅ **Dissolves.** `B` scores the *authoring* reversal (old label, new label, date, authority, reason — all written). `A`/`C` ask what the withdrawn label produced downstream. **Gate 9 asks what happens to *someone* the mechanism decides against; a reclassification decides against prior CLAIMS, not people.** ⇒ `B` is right on the gate; `A`/`C`'s question is real and is an **inheritance-hazard docket item**, not a Gate 9 fire |
| **`L84–86`** | FIRE | FIRE | PASS | ✅ **Dissolves** — three different questions, above |

## 1.3 · ⛔ `L127` — I MOVE. The pass call was mine and it was wrong.

**My Round 1 ground:** `L127` writes the unfavorable side (*"No living environmental knowledge survived that
chain of handoffs"* · *"Learning from a written record isn't the same as being taught by a living institution"*)
**and a route back with its cost** (*"the exiles still built their own practical mastery of the terrain
independently, over generations of their own"*).

**The rule, quoted exactly** — `04_QA_Gates_and_Differentiation.md` L216–219:

> **"For every finding describing a threshold, gate, conversion, verdict, admission or status change: *the
> mechanism runs both ways — did the file write both?* Ask what happens to someone the mechanism decides
> **against**, whether that outcome is as durable, and **whether there is any route back.** A 'no route back'
> nobody has perceived as a problem is a textbook shadow."**

⛔ **What changed my mind:** ***building a substitute is not a route back.*** The gate asks whether the mechanism
can be undone for the party it decided against. The chain of handoffs cannot be re-established; the teaching
institution is gone permanently; **nothing anyone at Davis can do restores it.** *"Built their own practical
mastery"* describes a **replacement**, and I scored a replacement as a return. `A` (`A-5`) and `B` (Fire 7) both
had this right and I did not.

⭐ **But the half of my Round 1 call that survives is worth keeping, because it sharpens the fire rather than
softening it:** `L127` **is** the only inherited site that writes *any* negative side. It writes the negative
**CONDITION** and stops before the negative **CONSEQUENCE** (what generations of reading without a teacher cost,
and who paid) and before the **route back** (there is none). ⇒ **`L127` is not a borderline fire. It is the most
informative fire in the corpus, precisely because it is the only one that got halfway and then stopped at
exactly the point `04` L219 names.**

## 1.4 · ⭐ THE AGREED LOAD-BEARING SET — what actually constrains Step 2

**A count is not the finding.** What follows is filtered by three tests, each taken from the gate's own wording:
**(i)** the mechanism decides about **someone**, not about a label, a file or an authoring act; **(ii)** the
unfavorable side is genuinely **unwritten**, not merely out of contract; **(iii)** the answer would change **what
this place is**, not what the file says about it.

# ITEM 2 · `01` §5.1'S CLASSES — THREE DIAGNOSES

## 2.1 · VERDICT

> ## ⛔ **THREE SEPARATE DEFECTS. Not one defect seen three ways. They have different causes, different scopes, and different owners — and only two of them are defects in the instrument at all.**
>
> ## ⭐ **But they share one consequence, and the consequence is the deliverable: *a Step 1 at this location can confidently assign TWO of the four classes.***

| | The diagnosis | Cause | Scope | Is `01` §5.1 at fault? |
|---|---|---|---|:-:|
| **`A`'s `D-5`** | **No class for an origination that AGES INTO an inheritance.** Terrain mastery is `Originated` in Act 1 and functionally locally-`Determined` by Act 2 | **The classes are defined relative to the parent and are time-invariant, while `M-157` says the call is act-dependent** | Any location with a long frame | ✅ **YES** |
| **`B`'s `D1`** | **`Originated` is UN-ASSIGNABLE in-run** — L413 requires differentiation against siblings, which ONE LOCATION forbids | **A clause inside `01` that a LATER law (2026-09-06) did not reach** | **Every city in the 38-city run** | ✅ **YES** |
| **`C`'s `B.0a`** *(mine)* | **`Aggregated` is UNAVAILABLE**, blocked by `DR-4`'s deferral of decomposition | **A developer ruling on THIS pass interacting with `01` §2.2's 3→4 threshold** | This location, and any deferred-decomposition location | ⛔ **NO — the instrument is fine. This is the cost of a ruling** |

⭐ **The distinction that makes them three and not one:** `A`'s and `B`'s survive the removal of `DR-4`; `C`'s
does not. `B`'s survives the removal of `M-157`; `A`'s does not. **No two of them fail together.**

## 2.2 · `B`'s finding is REAL and `B`'s conclusion is OVER-CONSERVATIVE. I MOVE, partially.

**The rule, quoted exactly** — `01_Frame_Typology_and_Inheritance.md` L413:

> **"| Originated | Exists here, comes from nowhere above. Must be differentiated against siblings | Claiming
> origination for something inherited — which is how two siblings end up 'independently' inventing the same
> custom. |"**

⭐⭐ **`B`'s finding is sharper than `B` stated it, and I am adopting it in the stronger form.** The ONE LOCATION
resolution enumerates the comparison instruments it relocates to the terminal check: *`03` §5b · Gate 6's
against-siblings half · Step 6 + the table · `02` §4's three-step rule · `02` §G8's z-score.* **`01` §5.1's
`Originated` clause is not among them.** ⇒ ***It is a SIXTH comparison instrument that the 2026-09-06 ruling did
not enumerate and therefore did not relocate.*** That is a live, run-wide methodology gap and it is `B`'s, not
mine — I did not see it in Round 1.

⛔ **But `B`'s conclusion does not follow from it.** Read the cell in halves: *"Exists here, comes from nowhere
above"* is the **definition**, and it is **parent-facing** — runnable in-run, at Davis, today. *"Must be
differentiated against siblings"* is the **validation**, and the error it names — *two siblings independently
inventing the same custom* — is a **convergence** problem, which is exactly what the terminal differentiation
check exists to catch. ⇒ **The class is assignable on its definition; only its validation is deferred — to
precisely where every other comparison instrument was already sent.** `00_Frame.md` §0.1 already does this for
Gate 6 (*"Gate 6 moves to Step 7"*) without anyone calling Gate 6 un-runnable.

> ### ⇒ **`Originated` IS ASSIGNABLE, AS `PROVISIONALLY-ORIGINATED`, WITH SIBLING VALIDATION DEFERRED TO THE TERMINAL CHECK.**

**What `B`'s reading would cost, concretely:** `B` hands Step 2 the instruction *"Step 2 must not select a
generator whose output requires an `Originated` call."* At Davis that is not a small guard — it would strike
**`G3`'s research half**, which is the one element `C`'s ledger and `A`'s §B.3 both independently reach by
different routes, and it would empty a class `01` L405 says every cultural element must occupy exactly one of.
⛔ **A deferred validation treated as a disqualification converts a methodology gap into missing content.**

## 2.3 · `A`'s finding — ADOPTED. I did not make it and it is correct.

⭐ **`A`'s `D-5` is a second edge of `M-157` that the `M-157` warning itself does not mention.** The warning
(`01` L398–402) says the **parent's** `Determined` class widens so `Inflected` and `Originated` narrow. **It does
not say that a location's OWN origination converts into its own later residents' inheritance.** For a resident
of 2750, terrain mastery is not something their city invented; it is something their city **has**, with no living
author. ✅ **`A` handled it correctly — `Originated` (Act 1) / functionally locally-`Determined` (Act 2), no
fifth class invented.** That is NO FORCED FIT applied in the direction people forget.

⚠ **And I add the instance I found that neither names, because it is a third edge:** the class can stay fixed
while the **content inverts** (my `D-5`) — the library welcome is `Inflected` in both Acts, but an Act 1
community wanting continuity with a home and an Act 2 properly-Tepenian community wanting the same books are not
doing the same thing. ⇒ **Three failure modes, one warning. `M-157` currently names one of them.**

## 2.4 · `Aggregated` — I HOLD, with one precision added

**The rule, quoted exactly** — `01` L414: *"Ignoring it produces a Band 5–6 polity written as if it were a large
Band 3 town."* And `01` L544: *"A Band 4+ pass that answers everything as Uniform has not been written at its own
scale."* Davis is declared **Band 5** with `01` §2.2's 3→4 decomposition **deferred by `DR-4`**.

✅ **`A` and `B` reached the same unavailability independently** (`A` §B.4: *"EMPTY BY DEFERRAL, not by absence…
`01` §5.4's declaration cannot run at all"*; `B` §B.4: *"Davis's children are NONE by deferral… the emptiness is
a result"*). **So this is a 3/3 convergence the brief filed as a divergence.** What is mine and what I hold is
the consequence: ***`DR-4` does not only defer distributions — it removes one of the four inheritance classes,
and it removes precisely the one whose omission `01` L414 says produces the band error.***

⚠ **Precision I owe, which my Round 1 phrasing blurred:** the class is unavailable **downward** (Davis as a
parent of children it may not have). **Upward it is legal and all three readers recorded it** — Davis's food
output and its position on the highway are inputs to the subnet's aggregate character. ⛔ **But `01` §5.2 rule 5
is `NOT DONE` and there is no Mirny registry to write into**, so the one live direction of the class has nowhere
to be recorded. ⭐ **Mitigation, and all three readers gesture at it: `01` §5.2 rule 5 and the upward `Aggregated`
obligation are ONE obligation, not two, and should be discharged together by the pass owner.**

## 2.5 · ⇒ WHICH CLASSES A STEP 1 AT DAVIS CAN ACTUALLY ASSIGN

| Class | Assignable? | Terms |
|---|:-:|---|
| **Determined** | ✅ **YES** | ⚠ **With a per-call ancestor stamp.** `01` §5.1 says *"the parent,"* singular; Davis has **two levels with different epistemic status** — the unwritten subnet (`UNKNOWN`, `PA-6`) and the Federation above it (knowable, out of contract). ⭐ **`A` (`D-4`) and `C` (`D-3`) found this independently and resolved it identically** — call it `Determined`, the conservative value, and name the ambiguity. **Unstamped, a `Determined` verdict launders the unwritten subnet's UNKNOWN into a fact, which is the exact failure `PA-6` exists to prevent.** ⚠ **This will recur on every city in the run whose subnet is unwritten** |
| **Inflected** | ✅ **YES — and this is where the pass's content is** | `01` L412: *"This is where most good local culture actually lives."* L416: *"the workhorse and is systematically under-used."* ⭐ **Unanimous across all three readers, by three different routes.** The slots are open and empty of local content: what each origin community does here · the library welcome · the social establishments · the arts community · founding-wave observance · maritime working life |
| **Originated** | ⚠ **YES, PROVISIONALLY** | Validation deferred to the terminal check (§2.2). **Two candidates at Davis: the limnological/hypersaline-lake vocation (solid — it rests on `L143`/`L154`/`L176`) and the terrain mastery at `L127` (conditional on ITEM 3).** ⚠ `A` additionally names a case that is `Inflected` in form and `Originated` in object and takes the conservative value rather than inventing a class — correct handling |
| **Aggregated** | ⛔ **NO, downward** | Blocked by ruling, not absent. **Upward-only, provisional, and currently unregisterable** |

## 2.6 · WHAT THE UNAVAILABLE CLASS COSTS STEP 2 — said plainly, not quietly assigned

⛔ **Step 2 cannot assert internal variation and cannot deny it.** Every Davis-internal claim this pass makes
reads as **Uniform** by default — and `01` L544 says a Band 4+ pass that answers everything as Uniform *"has not
been written at its own scale."* ⇒ **The pass will produce, by construction, the exact artifact `01` L414 names
as the `Aggregated` class's own failure mode, and it will produce it while correctly following a binding
ruling.** ✅ `00_Frame` §0.1 Configuration (a) already declares this as an EXCEPTIONAL property and lists the
finding-classes that depend on it. **Step 2's obligation is to tag against that list, not to work around it.**
⛔ **I do not propose lifting `DR-4`. The cost is stated so that it is on the record and so that the eventual
sub-location pass knows what to re-check.**

# ITEM 3 · `L127` AND THE GPS LINEAGE CLAUSE

## 3.1 · VERDICT

> ## ⛔ **NECESSARILY A DEVELOPER RULING. A pass cannot resolve it, for two independent reasons, and either one is sufficient.**
>
> ## ⭐ **But the question as currently posed is the wrong question, and reframing it makes it answerable in one sentence.**

**Why no pass can resolve it:**

1. ⛔ **The disputed material is OUT OF FRAME.** `L127`'s succession runs *"through the First Interwar Period"* —
   **2083–2564**. `00_Frame.md` §0.1 declares this pass's temporal frame as the **Second Interwar, 2564–2812**,
   with the First Interwar named only as *"Prior era."* **A pass cannot adjudicate the admissibility of material
   in an era it has declared out of frame, using canon roots that are not in its admissible set.**
2. ⛔ **The line carries a DATED adjudication under the same law.** `L127`'s parenthetical is labeled
   *"(Refined 2026-07-25 — GPS-purposes-only pass: …)"*. **A later pass re-applying the same law to a line a
   dated pass already cleared under that law is not adjudication, it is a second opinion.** ⭐ **Only the
   developer can say whether the 2026-07-25 refinement was made with the later lineage clarification in view.**
   ⚠ **Checkable and relevant: the refinement predates the 2026-09-06 developer rulings block by roughly six
   weeks. The lineage clarification's own date is not in my admissible set and I do not assert it.**

## 3.2 · ⭐⭐ THE REFRAMING — and it is the sharpest thing I can hand the developer

**The rule, as my brief states its scope:** *GPS-purposes-only covers a real site's builders, flag, lineage,
abandonment and fate — **provenance, not ethnicity.** The law exists so that a city is characterized by who lives
there, not by whose station it occupies.*

**Now read `L127` in its two halves, which is what no prior step did:**

| Half | Text | Status |
|---|---|---|
| **(i) REAL provenance** | *"on Davis Station infrastructure. The Australian Antarctic Division operated here from 1957."* | ✅ **Pure GPS coordinate. Explicitly allowed, uncontested by any reader.** `B` tiers the same fact at `L62` as *"GPS coordinate only"* |
| **(ii) IN-FICTION history** | *"through the First Interwar Period, the station was continuously maintained by a rotating succession of national operators… preserved journals, logs, and orientation manuals left behind across the centuries"* | ⛔ **THE DISPUTED HALF — and it is dated to 2083–2564, which is fictional time.** The real site has no 481-year Tepenian-universe custodial record; **this is invented setting history sited at a coordinate** |

> ## ⇒ **THE QUESTION, IN ONE SENTENCE:**
> # ***Does the GPS law's exclusion of a real site's lineage reach an IN-FICTION custodial history invented for that site — or does the law govern only the real record, leaving invented First-Interwar history admissible like any other worldbuilding?***
>
> **Concretely, at `Specs/Davis.md` L127: is the "rotating succession of national operators" (a) invented
> Tepenian history that happens to occupy a real coordinate, and therefore admissible; or (b) the real station's
> operator lineage carried forward with the names removed, and therefore excluded?**

⭐ **And the developer already has this question on the docket in a different costume.** `00_Frame.md` §0.7 files:
*"`01` §1.2 — does `Resettled` require a prior resident **POPULATION** or merely prior **OCCUPANCY**? (Settles
most of the 38-city run — nearly all sit on real station sites)."* ⇒ ***These are the same question from two
sides, and answering either answers both. They should go to the developer together, as one ruling.***

## 3.3 · THE BLAST RADIUS — the readers disagree, and it runs in BOTH directions

⛔ **Nobody costed the (a) branch. Everyone assumed a ruling can only subtract.**

### If (b) — INADMISSIBLE

| | Falls | Confidence |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | `G4`'s content collapses to `L129`'s bare *"Australian exiles,"* which is `Determined` | **3/3 would agree** |
| **2** | `00_Frame` §0.7's **`G2`×`G4` generator conflict loses its second term** | Certain — §0.7 names `L127` as the term |
| **3** | ⚠ **`PA-1` loses its preferred local constraint.** `00_Frame` §0.6 mitigates `PA-1`'s unsafe-from-2564 caveat with *"✅ Prefer `L127`'s documentary-inheritance founding, which needs no subnet at all."* **Remove `L127` and `PA-1`'s act-caveat becomes load-bearing instead of mitigated.** ⭐ **NEW — no reader noticed this** | Certain, from §0.6 as written |
| **4** | `LB-3` above dies; `A`'s sole `Originated` item dies | Certain |
| **5** | ⛔ **My Round 1 said *"`Originated` may empty entirely."* THAT IS WRONG AND I WITHDRAW IT.** The limnological vocation rests on `L143`/`L154`/`L176` and is **untouched** by any `L127` ruling. **`Originated` drops from two candidates to one; it does not empty** | **I MOVE** |
| **6** | ✅ **`00_Frame` §0.1a's `Resettled` decline SURVIVES.** §0.1a states *"Three independent grounds, any one sufficient."* Ground 2 cites `L127` and falls with it; **grounds 1 and 3 are untouched** | Certain, from §0.1a as written |
| **7** | ✅ **THE PASS DOES NOT BLOCK.** Five countable generators remain against a threshold of three | Certain |

### If (a) — ADMISSIBLE ⭐ *the branch nobody costed*

- ✅ `L127` stands; `G4` keeps its content; `LB-3` stands; the `G2`×`G4` conflict stands.
- ⚠⚠ **BUT `00_Frame` §0.1a's `Resettled` decline needs re-checking on ground 1.** Ground 1 reads: *"the only
  route to a 'first population' here runs through the real station's prior occupancy, which GPS-purposes-only
  excludes."* ⛔ **Ruling (a) supplies exactly what ground 1 says does not exist** — a prior occupying population
  at the site, inside the fiction, for 481 years. **Ground 1 does not survive ruling (a).**
- ⚠ **What would then hold the decline up: ground 2 alone, plus ground 3** — and ground 2 is itself under `B`'s
  `D10` challenge (*`00_Frame` calls the condition "explicitly superseded" on the strength of "built their own
  practical mastery"; **mastery being built is not misreadings being corrected**, and supersession of a starting
  point is not correction of its errors*). ⭐ **`B` filed that flag for a different purpose and it becomes
  load-bearing on this branch.**
- ⇒ **The decline probably still holds on grounds 2 and 3, but on a narrower base than §0.1a claims, and
  §0.1a's *"three independent grounds"* becomes *two.*** **That re-check is owed on the (a) branch and nobody
  has filed it.**

**ITEM 3 · What the losing reading costs.** ⛔ **Treating this as pass-resolvable in either direction is the
expensive error.** Resolving it as *"`L127` is fine, a dated pass cleared it"* leaves the pass's declared
sharpest single input, two selected generators' worth of content and a filed generator conflict standing on a
clearance that may have been issued under a narrower reading of the law. Resolving it as *"`L127` violates GPS,
strike it"* **deletes ratified-root content on a reader's application of a binding law to a line a dated pass
already adjudicated** — and, per §3.3(a), would do so without noticing that it also reopens a 3–0 ruling from
Step 0. ✅ **Filing it as a single developer question, merged with §0.7's existing item, costs the pass nothing:
Step 2 can proceed on `G2` and `G8` while it is open, tagging every `G4` finding as conditional.**

It is not usable as a fact about Davis"* | ✅ used; guarded under ONE LOCATION, **not under GPS** |
| **`L62`/`L64`** AAD 1957, the namesake's role | ✅ GPS coordinate | ✅ GPS coordinate | ✅ GPS coordinate |

> ### VERDICT: ⛔ **`B` IS RIGHT ON `L131`, AND `A` AND I BOTH BUILT A TOP-THREE FIRE ON MATERIAL `B` CORRECTLY FENCED OFF. I MOVE.**

**The rule, quoted exactly** — `00_Frame.md` §0.5 reserved item 1: *"the spec names a real historical person as
the city's namesake (L64, L131) — **that is site provenance under `G7`.** Building any in-world figure,
honorific, holiday or founding story on it would **invent a person AND violate GPS in the same sentence**."*

✅ **`B` attached that guard to its Saint fire explicitly and derived the correct admissible residue:** ***the
finding is a property of the SAINT ROSTER'S ADMISSION RULE — which is in-world — and never a Davis custom
honoring the man.***
⛔ **My own Round 1 `FIRE 3` Q4 asked *"Is the exclusion of enablers from Sainthood something residents of Davis
notice?"*** — which routes a Davis practice through the real namesake's biography and his standing in real

guard two lines below.** `A`'s `A-1` closing question (*"does Davis's namesake occupy it"*) has the same shape.

⇒ ✅ **Adopted for the load-bearing set (`E-2`): the admissible residue is the roster's admission criterion —

## ITEM 5 · ⭐ THE SAME LINE IS FILED IN THREE DIFFERENT EPISTEMIC TIERS

| Reader | Where `L127` sits |
|---|---|
| **`A`** | `C-1` **RATIFIED CANON** |
| **`B`** | **BARE ASSERTION** — *"`L127` 'No living environmental knowledge survived' — bare AND total"* |
| **`C`** | **Tier C PRIOR DERIVATION**, with the finding *"the pass's declared 'sharpest single input' is Tier C, not Tier A"* |

> ### VERDICT: ⛔ **ALL THREE ARE PARTLY WRONG, AND THE CORRECT ANSWER IS AN INSTRUMENT `B` BUILT AND DID NOT APPLY HERE.** I MOVE.

`B`'s own tier table carries **`⚠ RATIFIED ROOT, UNVERIFIED CONTENT`** — built for the climate block, on the
reasoning ***"Ratified root ≠ verified content."*** ⭐ **That is exactly `L127`'s status:** the host file is a
ratified root; the disputed content is explicitly labeled the output of a dated pass (*"Refined 2026-07-25"*).
⇒ **`L127` is RATIFIED ROOT / PASS-DERIVED CONTENT.** Not developer-ratified canon (`A` overstates its
durability); not bare assertion (`B` understates its provenance); `C` is closest but misses that the root is
ratified.

⭐ **Why this is not bookkeeping:** it fixes the **revision cost** of the pass's most load-bearing line, and it
decides ITEM 3's admissibility question. **A pass-derived refinement inside a ratified root is precisely the
class a later ruling CAN revise** — which is what makes ITEM 3 a live question rather than an attack on canon.

## ITEM 6 · ⚠⚠ THE COUNTS-ONLY PROBE — ONE OBSERVATION, THREE OPPOSITE HANDLINGS, AND THE HAZARD WAS COMMITTED IN THE SAME ROUND IT WAS PREDICTED

| Reader | Position | Probes run on the quarantined file |
|---|---|---|
| **`A`** | ⭐ **Offers it as a REUSABLE TECHNIQUE** — *"a counts-only probe can eliminate candidate readings of a match without reading it… Offered for the quarantine toolkit"* | ~7 string-candidate eliminations |
| **`C`** | ⚠ **Flags it as a HAZARD** (`D-8`) — *"a sequence of increasingly specific `grep -c` probes reconstructs the line's content without ever 'opening' it."* **Ran two, deliberately stopped** | 2, with a stated stopping rule |
| **`B`** | Declares `-c` only, in good faith | ⛔ **Measured the line's SHAPE** — bullet marker, **11 words**, **79 characters**, a comma immediately after the name, no 4-digit number anywhere — **then inferred its content class:** *"a shape consistent with an enumeration item and not with a dated event entry"* |

> ### VERDICT: ⛔ **BOTH `A` AND `C` ARE RIGHT, AND `B` DEMONSTRATED `C`'s HAZARD WHILE `A` WAS RECOMMENDING THE TECHNIQUE. The quarantine held in letter and leaked in substance.** I HOLD, now with evidence rather than prediction.

**The distinction that reconciles them, and it is operational:** **eliminating a candidate reading** (*is the
match the string `Davis Station`? no*) removes possibilities from a declared list. **Measuring the line's
shape** (*length, word count, punctuation position, absence of digits*) **reconstructs the artifact**, and `B`
converted that reconstruction into a content inference. ⛔ **`M-223` says *a command that returns lines returns
content* and endorses `-c`. It needs the corollary: *a SEQUENCE of counts returns content too, and a MEASUREMENT
returns more of it than an elimination does.***

⛔ **Concrete cost to this pass, and it is immediate:** `B`'s inference — *the match is an enumeration item, not
a dated event* — **is reconstructed content, not an admitted reading, and Step 2 must not rely on it.** It points
toward the same verdict the pass wants (`G6` null survives), which is exactly what makes it dangerous: **it is a
plausible answer obtained by a route the contract forbids, arriving in agreement with the prior.**
✅ **`REQ-G6-b` is unaffected: one line, one read contract, three values.** That remains the only legitimate route.

# ONE THING ALL THREE READERS PRODUCED AND ONLY ONE WROTE DOWN

⭐ **`A`'s §A.3 structural result is correct, is not contradicted by anything in `B` or `C`, and neither `B` nor
`C` stated it:**

> ### ***Davis's inherited material documents what ARRIVES and is near-silent on what leaves or is turned away.***

**Exiles arrive · documents arrive · twenty-one communities arrive · a highway arrives · a harbor admits ·
smuggled books are welcomed · precipitation falls · food is produced. Every one is written as an intake with no
refusal condition.** The one enormous outflow — 376,718 people — is recorded **as a number and not as a
mechanism**, and the one thing genuinely written as leaving is **water**, which the file subtracts from the
ledger without asking where it goes. ✅ **I endorse this as the audit's structural result and recommend it be
carried into Step 2 as such — it is the single sentence that explains why the load-bearing set has the shape it
has.** ⛔ **It is an audit result, not a culture finding; converting it is Step 2's work, not Step 1's.**
